## BookPage

**Type** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1917).

A page from a book, used for lectern display.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type BookPage = Message<"soulfire.v1.BookPage"> & {
  /**
   * Zero-indexed page number.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 page_number = 1;
   */
  pageNumber: number;

  /**
   * Page content text.
   * May contain Minecraft formatting codes (section symbols).
   *
   * @generated from field: string content = 2;
   */
  content: string;
};
```

### `pageNumber`

Zero-indexed page number.

**Type:** `number`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1923)

### `content`

Page content text.
May contain Minecraft formatting codes (section symbols).

**Type:** `string`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1931)

## BotConnectionPhase

**Enum** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3943).

Fine-grained connection phase for a bot, more detailed than the is_online
boolean. SPAWNED implies the player entity exists and live_state is available.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
enum BotConnectionPhase
```

### `UNSPECIFIED`

Unknown phase (e.g. for a bot that has never been started).

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.UNSPECIFIED`.

**Value:** `0`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3949)

### `CONNECTING`

The bot has been created and is establishing its connection / logging in,
but the player has not spawned yet.

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.CONNECTING`.

**Value:** `1`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3957)

### `CONNECTED`

The bot is connected to the server (post-login) but has not fully spawned.

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.CONNECTED`.

**Value:** `2`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3964)

### `SPAWNED`

The bot's player has spawned; live_state reflects the player.

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.SPAWNED`.

**Value:** `3`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3971)

### `DIED`

The bot's player is currently dead, awaiting respawn.

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.DIED`.

**Value:** `4`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3978)

### `DISCONNECTED`

The bot is not connected to a server.

**Type:** `BotConnectionPhase.DISCONNECTED`.

**Value:** `5`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L3985)

```ts
type BotListEntry = Message<"soulfire.v1.BotListEntry"> & {
  /**
   * The Minecraft profile UUID of the bot.
   * This uniquely identifies the bot within the instance.
   *
   * @generated from field: string profile_id = 1;
   */

   * reported a latency value for it.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional int32 ping_ms = 4;
   */
  pingMs?: number | undefined;

  /**
   * Fine-grained connection phase. Where is_online is a coarse boolean, this
   * distinguishes connecting / connected / spawned / disconnected states.
   *
   * @generated from field: soulfire.v1.BotConnectionPhase connection_phase = 5;

  /**

   * Absent if the name is unknown.

   * @generated from field: optional string account_name = 6;

  /**

   * @generated from field: soulfire.v1.BotStatus status = 7;
   */

};
```

Real-time state of a connected bot.
Contains position, health, inventory, and other live game data.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/browser`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/node`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/bun`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type BotLiveState = Message<"soulfire.v1.BotLiveState"> & {
  /**
   * X coordinate in the Minecraft world (east/west axis).
   * East is positive, West is negative.
   *
   * @generated from field: double x = 1;
   */
  x: number;

  /**
   * Y coordinate in the Minecraft world (vertical axis).
   * Increases upward. Sea level is typically around 63.
   *
   * @generated from field: double y = 2;
   */
  y: number;

  /**
   * Z coordinate in the Minecraft world (north/south axis).
   * South is positive, North is negative.
   *
   * @generated from field: double z = 3;
   */
  z: number;

  /**
   * Vertical rotation (pitch) in degrees.
   * Range: -90 (looking straight up) to 90 (looking straight down).
   * 0 is looking at the horizon.
   *
   * @generated from field: float xRot = 4;
   */
  xRot: number;

  /**
   * Horizontal rotation (yaw) in degrees.
   * Range: -180 to 180. 0 = South, 90 = West, -90 = East, +/-180 = North.
   *
   * @generated from field: float yRot = 5;
   */
  yRot: number;

  /**
   * Current health points.
   * Range: 0 (dead) to max_health. 1 point = half a heart in the UI.
   *
   * @generated from field: float health = 6;
   */
  health: number;

  /**
   * Maximum health points the player can have.
   * Default is 20 (10 hearts). Can be modified by effects or attributes.
   *
   * @generated from field: float max_health = 7;
   */
  maxHealth: number;

  /**
   * Current hunger/food level.
   * Range: 0 (starving) to 20 (full). 1 point = half a drumstick in the UI.
   * Below 6, the player cannot sprint. At 0, health starts draining.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 food_level = 8;
   */
  foodLevel: number;

  /**
   * Current saturation level.
   * Range: 0 to food_level. Acts as a buffer before hunger depletes.
   * When saturation is above 0, hunger bar doesn't decrease while performing actions.
   *
   * @generated from field: float saturation_level = 9;
   */
  saturationLevel: number;

  /**
   * Inventory slots containing items (only non-empty slots are included).
   * For list views (GetBotList), this will be empty for performance.
   * For detail views (GetBotInfo), contains all non-empty slots from the player inventory.
   *
   * @generated from field: repeated soulfire.v1.InventorySlot inventory = 10;
   */
  inventory: InventorySlot[];

  /**
   * Currently selected hotbar slot index.
   * Range: 0 to 8, corresponding to hotbar slots 1-9 in the UI.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 selected_hotbar_slot = 11;
   */
  selectedHotbarSlot: number;

  /**
   * The dimension the player is currently in.
   * Format: namespaced identifier (e.g., "minecraft:overworld", "minecraft:the_nether", "minecraft:the_end").
   * Custom dimensions will use their server-defined namespace.
   *
   * @generated from field: string dimension = 12;
   */
  dimension: string;

  /**
   * Player's current experience level (the number shown on the XP bar).
   * Range: 0 to theoretically unlimited (typically 0-30+ for enchanting).
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 experience_level = 13;
   */
  experienceLevel: number;

  /**
   * Progress towards the next experience level.
   * Range: 0.0 (just reached current level) to 1.0 (about to level up).
   *
   * @generated from field: float experience_progress = 14;
   */
  experienceProgress: number;

  /**
   * Skin texture hash for avatar rendering.
   * This is the hash extracted from the texture URL in the player's game profile.
   * Can be used with services like mc-heads.net: https://mc-heads.net/body/<hash>
   * Will be absent if the player has no custom skin or skin data is unavailable.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string skin_texture_hash = 15;
   */
  skinTextureHash?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * Current game mode of the player.
   * Determines available actions (breaking blocks, flying, taking damage, etc.).
   *
   * @generated from field: soulfire.v1.GameMode game_mode = 16;
   */
  gameMode: GameMode;
};
```

```ts
   * Default: 854 (if 0 or not provided).

  maxDistance?: number | undefined;

  /**

  xRot?: number | undefined;
```

## BotService

**Object** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4341).

Service for managing and controlling Minecraft bots.
Provides comprehensive APIs for bot monitoring, movement control,
inventory management, and interaction with server dialogs.

Permissions:
- READ_BOT_INFO: Required for GetBotList, GetBotInfo, GetInventoryState,
                 RenderBotPov, GetDialog
- CONTROL_BOTS: Required to start, stop, or restart bots
- UPDATE_BOT_CONFIG: Required for persistent per-bot setting changes
- CONTROL_BOT_ACTIONS: Required for live movement, inventory, and dialog actions

Error Handling:
- NOT_FOUND: Instance or bot does not exist
- FAILED_PRECONDITION: Bot is not online or required game state is unavailable
- INTERNAL: Unexpected server errors (check server logs for details)

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

Each field is optional to allow partial updates - only specified fields are changed.
The movement state persists until explicitly changed or reset.

```ts
  /**
   * The profile UUID of the bot to control.
   *
   * @generated from field: string bot_id = 2;
   */
  botId: string;

  /**
   * W key state - move forward.
   * true = pressing W, false = not pressing W
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool forward = 3;
   */
  forward?: boolean | undefined;

  /**
   * S key state - move backward.
   * true = pressing S, false = not pressing S
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool backward = 4;
   */
  backward?: boolean | undefined;

  /**
   * A key state - strafe left.
   * true = pressing A, false = not pressing A
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool left = 5;
   */
  left?: boolean | undefined;

  /**
   * D key state - strafe right.
   * true = pressing D, false = not pressing D
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool right = 6;
   */
  right?: boolean | undefined;

  /**
   * Space key state - jump.
   * true = pressing Space, false = not pressing Space
   * When held, the bot will jump repeatedly.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool jump = 7;
   */
  jump?: boolean | undefined;

  /**
   * Shift key state - sneak/crouch.

   * @generated from field: optional bool sneak = 8;

   * true = sprinting, false = not sprinting
   * Requires forward movement and sufficient hunger (food level >= 6).
   *
   * @generated from field: optional bool sprint = 9;
   */
  sprint?: boolean | undefined;
};
```

## ClickType

**Enum** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4047).

Types of inventory click actions available in Minecraft.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/browser`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/node`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/bun`, `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
enum ClickType
```

### `CLICK_TYPE_UNSPECIFIED`

Invalid/unspecified click type. Will result in an error.

**Type:** `ClickType.CLICK_TYPE_UNSPECIFIED`.

**Value:** `0`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4053)

### `LEFT_CLICK`

Left mouse click on a slot.
- Empty cursor + item in slot: Pick up entire stack to cursor
- Item on cursor + empty slot: Place entire stack in slot
- Item on cursor + same item in slot: Combine stacks (up to max stack size)
- Item on cursor + different item in slot: Swap cursor and slot items

**Type:** `ClickType.LEFT_CLICK`.

**Value:** `1`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4064)

### `RIGHT_CLICK`

Right mouse click on a slot.
- Empty cursor + item in slot: Pick up half the stack (rounded up)
- Item on cursor + empty slot: Place one item from cursor
- Item on cursor + same item in slot: Place one item from cursor
- Item on cursor + different item in slot: Swap cursor and slot items

**Type:** `ClickType.RIGHT_CLICK`.

**Value:** `2`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4075)

### `SHIFT_LEFT_CLICK`

Shift + left click on a slot.
Quick-moves the item stack to the opposite inventory section.
- From player inventory to container (chest, furnace, etc.)
- From container to player inventory
- Between hotbar and main inventory

**Type:** `ClickType.SHIFT_LEFT_CLICK`.

**Value:** `3`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4086)

### `DROP_ONE`

Drop one item from the slot (Q key while hovering over slot).
Drops a single item from the stack, leaving the rest.

**Type:** `ClickType.DROP_ONE`.

**Value:** `4`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4094)

### `DROP_ALL`

Drop entire stack from the slot (Ctrl+Q while hovering over slot).
Drops all items from the stack at once.

**Type:** `ClickType.DROP_ALL`.

**Value:** `5`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4102)

### `SWAP_HOTBAR`

Swap slot contents with a hotbar slot (number keys 1-9).
Requires hotbar_slot field to specify which hotbar slot (0-8).
Works regardless of what's in the cursor.

**Type:** `ClickType.SWAP_HOTBAR`.

**Value:** `6`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4111)

### `MIDDLE_CLICK`

Middle mouse click (creative mode clone).
In creative mode, clones the item to cursor without affecting the slot.
No effect in survival mode.

**Type:** `ClickType.MIDDLE_CLICK`.

**Value:** `7`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4120)

## ContainerButton

**Type** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1801).

A clickable button in a container menu.
Used for special interactions like enchantment selection, stonecutter recipes, trade offers, etc.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type ContainerButton = Message<"soulfire.v1.ContainerButton"> & {
  /**
   * Button ID to send to the server when clicking.
   * The meaning depends on the container type.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 button_id = 1;
   */
  buttonId: number;

  /**
   * Display label for the button.
   * Examples: "Enchant Slot 1", "Recipe 5", "8x Emerald -> 1x Diamond Pickaxe"
   *
   * @generated from field: string label = 2;
   */
  label: string;

  /**
   * Optional item ID for displaying an icon.
   * Example: "minecraft:enchanted_book", "minecraft:stone_bricks"
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string icon_item_id = 3;
   */
  iconItemId?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * Optional tooltip/description text with additional details.
   * Examples: "Requires 3 lapis, 30 levels", "Out of stock"
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string description = 4;
   */
  description?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * Whether the button is currently disabled (cannot be clicked).
   * Reasons vary by container: missing materials, out of stock, insufficient levels, etc.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool disabled = 5;
   */
  disabled: boolean;

  /**
   * Whether this button is currently selected/active.
   * Used for toggle-style buttons or to show current selection.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool selected = 6;
   */
  selected: boolean;
};
```

### `buttonId`

Button ID to send to the server when clicking.
The meaning depends on the container type.

**Type:** `number`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1808)

### `label`

Display label for the button.
Examples: "Enchant Slot 1", "Recipe 5", "8x Emerald -&gt; 1x Diamond Pickaxe"

**Type:** `string`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1816)

### `iconItemId`

Optional item ID for displaying an icon.
Example: "minecraft:enchanted_book", "minecraft:stone_bricks"

**Type:** `string | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1824)

### `description`

Optional tooltip/description text with additional details.
Examples: "Requires 3 lapis, 30 levels", "Out of stock"

**Type:** `string | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1832)

### `disabled`

Whether the button is currently disabled (cannot be clicked).
Reasons vary by container: missing materials, out of stock, insufficient levels, etc.

**Type:** `boolean`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1840)

### `selected`

Whether this button is currently selected/active.
Used for toggle-style buttons or to show current selection.

**Type:** `boolean`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1848)

## ContainerLayout

**Type** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1947).

Complete layout description for an open container menu.
Provides all information needed to render the container UI.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type ContainerLayout = Message<"soulfire.v1.ContainerLayout"> & {
  /**
   * Container title/name displayed at the top.
   * Examples: "Chest", "Crafting Table", "Inventory", custom container names.
   *
   * @generated from field: string title = 1;
   */
  title: string;

  /**
   * Ordered list of slot regions defining the container layout.
   * Regions are listed in rendering order (typically container slots first, then player inventory).
   *
   * @generated from field: repeated soulfire.v1.SlotRegion regions = 2;
   */
  regions: SlotRegion[];

  /**
   * Total number of slots in the container.
   * Sum of all region slot_counts.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 total_slots = 3;
   */
  totalSlots: number;

  /**
   * Available action buttons for this container.
   * Present for: stonecutter (recipes), enchanting table (3 enchant options),
   * loom (patterns), villager trading (trade offers), beacon (effects), etc.
   *
   * @generated from field: repeated soulfire.v1.ContainerButton buttons = 4;
   */
  buttons: ContainerButton[];

  /**
   * Container type identifier for client-specific rendering.
   * Values: "inventory", "chest", "dispenser", "hopper", "furnace", "crafting",
   *         "anvil", "enchanting", "brewing", "beacon", "shulker", "grindstone",
   *         "stonecutter", "loom", "cartography", "smithing", "merchant",
   *         "crafter", "lectern", "generic"
   *
   * @generated from field: string container_type = 5;
   */
  containerType: string;

  /**
   * Text input fields available in this container.
   * Currently only populated for anvil menus.
   *
   * @generated from field: repeated soulfire.v1.ContainerTextInput text_inputs = 6;
   */
  textInputs: ContainerTextInput[];

  /**
   * Book pages for lectern containers.
   * Contains all pages of the book on the lectern.
   *
   * @generated from field: repeated soulfire.v1.BookPage book_pages = 7;
   */
  bookPages: BookPage[];

  /**
   * Current page being displayed (for lectern).
   * Zero-indexed into book_pages.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 current_book_page = 8;
   */
  currentBookPage: number;
};
```

**Referenced types:** [`SlotRegion`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/generated-soulfire-bot-pb#slotregion), [`ContainerButton`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/generated-soulfire-bot-pb#containerbutton), [`ContainerTextInput`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/generated-soulfire-bot-pb#containertextinput), [`BookPage`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/generated-soulfire-bot-pb#bookpage).

Container title/name displayed at the top.
Examples: "Chest", "Crafting Table", "Inventory", custom container names.

**Type:** `string`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1954)

### `regions`

Ordered list of slot regions defining the container layout.
Regions are listed in rendering order (typically container slots first, then player inventory).

**Type:** `SlotRegion[]` ([`SlotRegion`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/generated-soulfire-bot-pb#slotregion)).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L1962)

### `totalSlots`

Total number of slots in the container.

**Type:** `number`.

## ContainerTextInput

Text input field in a container menu.
Currently only used by the anvil for item renaming.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type ContainerTextInput = Message<"soulfire.v1.ContainerTextInput"> & {
  /**
   * Field identifier for the SetContainerText RPC.
   * Currently supported: "item_name" (anvil rename)
   *
   * @generated from field: string id = 1;
   */
  id: string;

  /**
   * Display label for the input field.
   *
   * @generated from field: string label = 2;
   */
  label: string;

  /**
   * Current text value in the field.
   * For anvil: the item's current custom name, or empty if none.
   *
   * @generated from field: string current_value = 3;
   */
  currentValue: string;

  /**
   * Maximum allowed character length.
   * For anvil: 50 characters.
   * Value of 0 means no limit.
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 max_length = 4;
   */
  maxLength: number;

  /**
   * Placeholder text shown when field is empty.
   *
   * @generated from field: string placeholder = 5;
   */
  placeholder: string;
};
```

### `id`

## InventorySlot

**Type** in `generated/soulfire/bot_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L416).

Represents an item in an inventory slot.
Used for both container slots and carried (cursor) items.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
type InventorySlot = Message<"soulfire.v1.InventorySlot"> & {
  /**
   * The slot index within the container.
   * - For container menus: 0 to (total_slots - 1)
   * - For player inventory: 0-4 crafting, 5-8 armor, 9-35 main inventory, 36-44 hotbar, 45 offhand
   * - For carried items (cursor): -1 is used as a special indicator
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 slot = 1;
   */
  slot: number;

  /**
   * The Minecraft item identifier in namespaced format.
   * Examples: "minecraft:diamond_sword", "minecraft:stone", "minecraft:enchanted_book"
   *
   * @generated from field: string item_id = 2;
   */
  itemId: string;

  /**
   * The number of items in this stack.
   * Range: 1 to the item's maximum stack size (typically 1, 16, or 64).
   *
   * @generated from field: int32 count = 3;
   */
  count: number;

  /**
   * The custom display name of the item, if it has been renamed.
   * Only present when the item has a custom name component (e.g., renamed in an anvil).
   * Contains the plain text string, not JSON text components.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string display_name = 4;
   */
  displayName?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * Base64-encoded square icon image for this item's inventory render.
   * The image is rendered server-side to match the item's resolved in-game appearance.
   * Static items are sent as PNG. Animated items may be sent as GIF.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string icon_base64 = 5;
   */
  iconBase64?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * MIME type for the rendered item icon image.
   * Values: "image/png" or "image/gif".
   *
   * @generated from field: optional string icon_mime_type = 6;
   */
  iconMimeType?: string | undefined;
};
```

### `slot`

The slot index within the container.
- For container menus: 0 to (total_slots - 1)
- For player inventory: 0-4 crafting, 5-8 armor, 9-35 main inventory, 36-44 hotbar, 45 offhand
- For carried items (cursor): -1 is used as a special indicator

**Type:** `number`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L425)

### `itemId`

The Minecraft item identifier in namespaced format.
Examples: "minecraft:diamond_sword", "minecraft:stone", "minecraft:enchanted_book"

**Type:** `string`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L433)

### `count`

The number of items in this stack.
Range: 1 to the item's maximum stack size (typically 1, 16, or 64).

**Type:** `number`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L441)

### `displayName`

The custom display name of the item, if it has been renamed.
Only present when the item has a custom name component (e.g., renamed in an anvil).
Contains the plain text string, not JSON text components.

**Type:** `string | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L450)

### `iconBase64`

Base64-encoded square icon image for this item's inventory render.
The image is rendered server-side to match the item's resolved in-game appearance.
Static items are sent as PNG. Animated items may be sent as GIF.

**Type:** `string | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L459)

### `iconMimeType`

MIME type for the rendered item icon image.
Values: "image/png" or "image/gif".

**Type:** `string | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L467)

Describes how a region of slots should be rendered in the UI.
Different region types have different visual treatments.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_pb`.

```ts
enum SlotRegionType
```

### `SLOT_REGION_NORMAL`

Regular interactive slots that can accept most items.

**Type:** `SlotRegionType.SLOT_REGION_NORMAL`.

**Value:** `0`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4141)

### `SLOT_REGION_OUTPUT`

Output-only slots (crafting result, furnace output).
Items can only be taken out, not placed in.

**Type:** `SlotRegionType.SLOT_REGION_OUTPUT`.

**Value:** `1`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4149)

### `SLOT_REGION_DISPLAY`

Display-only slots (lectern book display, some special UIs).
May not allow any direct interaction.

**Type:** `SlotRegionType.SLOT_REGION_DISPLAY`.

**Value:** `2`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4157)

### `SLOT_REGION_HOTBAR`

**Type:** `SlotRegionType.SLOT_REGION_HOTBAR`.

**Value:** `3`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4165)

### `SLOT_REGION_ARMOR`

Armor slots in player inventory.

**Type:** `SlotRegionType.SLOT_REGION_ARMOR`.

**Value:** `4`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_pb.ts#L4173)
